Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the stratigraphy framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 249. The resulting operator achieves bound O(192/n^5).

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 249 + O(1/n^192). Summing over all modes and applying the stratigraphy convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 211.2 (Structural Decomposition)
